      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "Calendar and Climate",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, the climate and the time are both very important factors. Let's start with the Calendar. At any time, you can view the calendar by pressing , and clicking on the calendar tab. This will show the Season, the Day, and the Date.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "The Calendar Screen",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "There are seasons, and the weather and climate will change along with them! There are four seasons in TerraFirmaCraft, each divided up into Early, Mid and Late months. The four seasons are: - Spring: March - May - Summer: June - August - Autumn: September - November - Winter: December - February",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "The current season can influence the temperature of the area, the precipitation (if it will rain or snow), among other things. Pay attention to the calendar tab, it will be useful! Now, onto the climate...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "Another tab on the main inventory screen is the Climate screen. This one shows information about the current location The first line shows the overall Climate . The second line shows the Geologic Province. The third line shows the Average Annual Temperature.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "The Climate Screen",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "Temperature in TerraFirmaCraft is influenced by a number of factors: - Firstly, the region, especially the latitude (Z coordinate) will play the largest role. - Secondly, the current season will influence the temperature - it will be hottest during Summer, and coldest during Winter. - Finally, the temperature can be different day to day as well as varying from hour to hour.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "The last line shows the current temperature, including all these aforementioned factors. Temperature can influence many things: if crops and plants will grow, if snow and ice will form or melt, and more.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "Rainfall is another climate value that can vary depending on where you are in the world. The annual rainfall is measured in millimeters (mm) and can be between 0mm - 500mm. Rainfall affects the types of flora that are found in an area, and also the types of soil, from sand and cacti, to loam, to silt and kapok trees.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/climate.json",
          "text": "Rainfall is also important as it affects what things can be grown in an area. Rainfall is one of the main contributors to Hydration, which is an exact measure of how wet the soil is in a given location, and is used by Crops, Fruit Trees, and Berry Bushes to determine if they can grow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Calendar and Climate",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Material List",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can undo with the exchanging gadget, but you must have the necessary items. For example, if you exchanged grass into stone bricks, you must have grass in your inventory to undo this exchange. Don't forget to apply silk touch if desired!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor allows you to 'lock in' the block you're looking at, so you can freely move or look around. There is a hotkey for this, or you can access it from the settings menu. You can right click at any time to build the structure when it is anchored. Note: Changing range may not take effect until you remove and replace the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor your render!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anchor",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Bind Inventory",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Binding to an inventory allows you to interact with that inventory to extract/insert items. First, enable 'bind inventory' mode in the settings menu of your gadget. Then shift-right click on a chest or other inventory. A blue box around the inventory will note your success. Shift-Right click a non inventory block (like grass) to disconnect.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Bind Inventory",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Binding to a Chest!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "You can also bind to an Applied Energistics 2 System by connecting to the wireless access point of that system. The gadget will have unlimited range, so no need to worry about range upgrades!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Bind Inventory",

          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_bindinv.json",
          "text": "Binding to AE2!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Bind Inventory",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_fluidhandling",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_fluidhandling.json",
          "text": "Fluid Handling",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Fluid Handling",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_fluidhandling",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_fluidhandling.json",
          "text": "Gadgets can interact with Fluids! Make sure Raytracing Fluids is enabled to select a fluid with shift-rightclick. To place fluids in the world, you'll need a bucket with the appropriate fluid in it. Alternatively, mods that add tanks (like Mekanism) can be used, and the fluids will be removed/inserted into tanks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Handling",
          "item_ref_count": 7,

    {
      "id": "tfc:pets",
      "label": "Pets",
      "origin": "namespace_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 10,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Pets are animals that function quite similar to Livestock, but have no useful products. Instead, they can obey commands, follow you around your home, control pests, and help you hunt.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Pets recognize a certain player as their Owner. To become a pet's owner, feed it to raise its familiarity above 15%%. Pets will only obey commands that come from their owner. Clicking on a pet with dye changes its collar color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "A dog.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Dogs are a pet that eats grains, vegetables, and meat, rotten or not rotten. They will help you hunt most any land predator or prey.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "A cat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Cats are a pet that eats grains, cooked meats, and dairy products. They will help you hunt land prey and small fish. When at home, they will kill pests without being asked to. Cats do not always listen when being told to sit.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Responsible pet owners must learn to control their pet. Luckily, this is easy. By pressing while holding with an empty hand, the Pet Screen may be opened. This screen contains a number of commands that can be used to tell the pet what to do: Relax, We're Home, Sit, Follow Me, and Hunt With Me.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Relax is the default state of pets, and commanding them returns them to this state. If a pet it at home, it will wander around the property, but not leave, and sleep once a day. If it is too far from home, it will walk aimlessly.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "We're Home sets the home position of the pet. This tells the animal that it should, when relaxing, stay in that general area. Telling an animal that it is at home does not change the activity it is currently doing. Sit tells the pet to sit where it is. However, animals have minds of their own, and will eventually get bored and stand up!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/pets.json",
          "text": "Hunt With Me tells the pet to follow you, and engage in combat when you attack or are attacked. Pets will only attack animals that they are able to (for example, cats cannot help attack bears!). Follow Me is the same as Hunt With Me, but the pet will not risk itself in combat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Pets",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian have different trades than earth villagers, featuring planet-specific materials, space suits and oxygen gear.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Lunarian Wandering Traders appear on every moon and planet. They have many rocket-related trades that may save your life, including launch pads, oxygen, fuel, soul torches and water. They accept emeralds, which are commonly found in the various structures on the moon and planets.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Wandering Trader",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Lunarians",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover is a land vehicle capable of driving on the rough terrain of moons and planets. It's exceptionally fast, has an inventory capable of storing 16 items and has two seats for multiple riders. You can also run over mobs and deal some serious damage. To use it, fill the rover with fuel (shift-click and place fuel buckets in the left input slot).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {

          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",

          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/rover.json",
          "text": "The rover has a built-in radio for playing real internet streamed radio stations. Use it by interacting with the radio on the rover or by pressing (). If multiple players are in the rover, they'll hear the same radio as well.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Rover",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Piglin Bartering",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Like you'd expect, you can trade with Piglins in the same way you trade with them in vanilla, with Gold Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "You can find Piglins in almost every biome throughout the Beneath.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Trade Results",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "- 1 Copper Ingot - 1 Tin Ingot - 1 Silver Ingot - 1 Bronze Ingot - 1 Bismuth Bronze Ingot - 1 Cast Iron Ingot - 1 Zinc Ingot - 1 Brass Ingot - 1 Bismuth Ingot - 1 Nickel Ingot - 1 Cinnabar Gem",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "- 4-6 Sulfur Powder - 1-2 Leather - 6-12 String - 6-12 Nether Quartz - 12-18 Mangrove Lumber - 6-8 Mangrove Logs - 4-6 Cooked Venison - 4-6 Ghost Peppers - 4-6 Gunpowder - 4-8 Charcoal - 6-12 Arrows - 6-12 Flux - 8-12 Pyroxenite Bricks - 8-12 Migmatite Bricks - 8-16 Sticks",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Raiding a Bastion might be very dangerous, but they can also be very rewarding! You can find things like: - Gold, Rose Gold, Pig Iron, Black Bronze, and Silver ingots - Black Bronze weapons, tools, and armor - Kaolin clay and graphite - Lanterns - Cloth, leather, seeds, and food - And other treasures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "Piglin Disguise",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/piglins.json",
          "text": "If you want to ensure an easy time trading--or simply mingling with the locals--Put on a Piglin Disguise to prevent Piglins from being hostile with you on first sight. But beware! They only put up with so much...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Piglins",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Animal Husbandry",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock are animals that can be tamed and bred by the player. Livestock can be either male or female. For some animals, it is possible to tell their sex visually. For example, male pigs have tusks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock experience aging. They are born as babies, which are smaller and cannot provide things for the player. After a certain number of days, they grow into adult animals, which are able to do things like breed or produce milk. After they breed or are used enough times, animals become old, and are only useful for their meat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "This bull is old and cannot breed, so it has a faded coat and grey, unseeing eyes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock can be fed to raise familiarity. Each animal has foods that it prefers to eat. To feed an animal, and with food in your hand.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Livestock have a Familiarity Indicator that shows how familiar they are with you. Hold and look at the animal to reveal it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Familiarity decays a little each day if not fed. Raising the familiarity enough prevents decay, indicated by the white outlined heart.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Adult livestock cannot be fully familiarized, indicated by the red outlined heart. Babies can reach 100% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Mammals are livestock that experience Pregnancy. Adult mammals that are above 30% familiarity and have been fed that day will mate, given that they are of opposite genders and near each other. The female animal will become pregnant, which causes it to have children a set number of days after fertilization. An example is Pigs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Wooly Animals are Mammals that can be Sheared if they are adults and familiar enough to you. Some examples are Sheep, Alpacas, and Musk Oxen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Dairy Animals are mammals that make Milk. Female dairy animals can be clicked with a bucket to obtain milk. Some examples are Goats, Cows, and Yaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Nest Box",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Oviparous Animals are not Mammals, and instead produce children by laying Eggs. They need a Nest Box to lay eggs, which they are capable of locating on their own. Some examples are Ducks, Quails, and Chickens.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Eggs can be cooked or boiled for food. Male oviparous animals can fertilize females, which causes the next egg laid in the nest box to be fertilized. Fertilized eggs will have a tooltip with how long until they are ready to hatch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Equines are Mammals that can be ridden when tamed. They become rideable after reaching 15% familiarity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "They need a Saddle to ride, which can be Knapped. This includes Mules, Donkeys, and Horses. Mules and Donkeys can hold any chest or barrel. If holding a barrel, while holding can be used to remove it. The same keys while holding a bucket can be used to drain fluid from the barrel. The next few pages will go over all livestock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Pigs spawn in mild forests with temperature between -10 and 35°C, and at least 200mm of rainfall. They are Mammals with no special abilities. They will eat any food, even if it is rotten. They have 1-10 children, are pregnant for just 19 days, and reach adulthood in 80 days. They can have children 6 times.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A pig.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Cows spawn in most climates, between temperature -10 and 35°C, and at least 250mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They only eat grains, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 58 days, and reach adulthood in 192 days. They can have children 13 times, if they are never milked, or be milked 128 times, if they are never bred. They produce milk every day.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A cow.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Goats spawn in moderate climates, with temperature between -12 and 25°C, and at least 300mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat grains, fruits, and vegetables, which may be rotten. They can have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 32 days, and reach adulthood in 96 days. They can have children 6 times if they are never milked, or be milked 60 times if they are never bred. They produce milk every 3 days.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A goat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Yaks spawn in cold climates, with temperature of at most -11°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Dairy Animals. They eat only fresh grains. They always have 1 child, are pregnant for 64 days, and reach adulthood in 180 days. They can have children 23 times, if they are never milked, or be milked 230 times, if they are never bred. They produce milk once a day.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A yak.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Alpacas spawn in moderate climates, with temperature between -8 and 20°C, and at least 250mm of rainfall. They are Wooly Animals. They eat grains and fruits. They have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 36 days, and reach adulthood in 98 days. They can have children 13 times, if they are never sheared, or be sheared 128 times, if they are never bred. They grow wool every 6 days.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "An alpaca.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "Sheep spawn in drier climates, with temperature between 0 and 35°C, and between 70 and 300mm of rainfall. They are Wooly Animals. They eat grains. They have 1-2 children, are pregnant for 32 days, and reach adulthood in 56 days. They can have children 6 times, if they are never sheared, or be sheared 60 times, if they are never bred. They grow wool every 9 days.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Animal Husbandry",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 40
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/animal_husbandry.json",
          "text": "A sheep.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",

          "text": "Musk Oxen spawn in cold climates, with temperature between -25 and 0°C, and at least 100mm of rainfall. They are Wooly Animals. They eat grains.
